In this framework, the meaning of the lexical-semantic relations between the premise and the hypothesis is reconfigured to achieve a more flexible semantic network that induces different reasoning paths in LLMs. These new pathways show a consistent pattern of responses that allows agreement on a single response. The results demonstrate that our proposal allows to discover and compensate for LLMs' semantic knowledge gaps in NLI, achieving significant improvements in accuracy, exceeding 10% for some models, and in particular for the non-entailment class. It is essential to note that LLMs need structured knowledge and not just more data to bridge reasoning gaps. Our hybrid approach directs attention to overlooked word relationships, allowing models to synthesize missing information. We believe that the future lies not in increasing model size, but in creating a semantic scafolding that mimics the flexibility of human thinking. Hopefully, our proposal will enable the development of more robust agents and interpretable reasoning, guiding AI toward reliable language understanding.

</details>


### [179] [PERSONAWEAVER: Controllable Diversity Beyond Conventional Archetypes in Procedural Character Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26629)
*Maan Qraitem,Kate Saenko,Bryan A. Plummer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的基于大语言模型（LLM）的虚拟角色生成方法——PersonaWeaver，能生成行为更加多样化的虚拟角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前利用LLM进行自动角色生成存在行为和价值观同质化问题，大多数生成的角色表现为遵循正面伦理及助人反应，缺乏多样性和丰富性。该研究旨在打破这一困境，使自动生成的虚拟角色更加真实、多样。

Method: 提出PersonaWeaver，将世界观设定和行为规范分离，通过手工构建的多样化道德立场与对话反应库来指导LLM生成不同类型角色，并在十个现实与幻想设定、三种主流LLM上进行测试。

Result: PersonaWeaver生成的角色在道德、互动反应等方面呈现出比以往方法更广泛的多样分布；语言、反应长度、情感也更丰富，角色不再局限于典型原型组合。

Conclusion: PersonaWeaver有效打破了LLM生成虚拟角色行为同质化的限制，提升了多样性，对相关虚拟世界内容生成具有重要意义。

Abstract: Procedural character generation aims to populate games, simulations, and other virtual worlds with diverse characters. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising foundation for scaling this task. However, LLM-based procedural character generation remains at an early stage: existing methods either generate characters directly or adapt profiles retrieved from persona banks. As we show, both approaches produce behaviorally homogeneous populations: characters overwhelmingly agree with positive moral norms and respond to questions with helpful, assistant-like reactions. To mitigate this homogenization, we introduce PersonaWeaver, which disentangles world building from behavioral specification and models behavior through setting general, diverse, manually curated banks of moral positions and conversational reactions. This design allows us to test how far LLM(s) can be pushed beyond their default behavioral patterns across settings. Across ten realistic and fantastical settings and three LLM(s), PersonaWeaver produces broader moral and interactional response distributions than prior work. Its guidance also diversifies interpersonal language, response length, and sentiment. It also produces less archetypal combinations of world attributes. Code is available at https://github.com/mqraitem/PersonaWeaver.

</details>


### [180] [Knowledge Pull Requests for Continual Document Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26634)
*Alexander Martin,Benjamin Van Durme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Knowledge Pull Requests（KPRs）框架，实现文档持续编写时对知识变更的可解释化处理。该框架通过结构化集成新知识，提高了内容更新的可追溯性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文档编辑在面对跨来源、语言、时间的新知识时，缺乏对知识变更的明晰追踪，大多仅直接编辑文本或完全重生成，无法充分利用增量知识、保障内容一致性。

Method: KPR框架通过抽取新的知识声明，把这些声明分配到文档相应部分，并检测与现有内容的冲突，生成详细的变更日志（ChangeLog），分离知识变更与文档文本差异。实验在跨语言Wikipedia维护及按需报告(RAGTIME)更新任务上进行。

Result: KPR比重写或完全重生成方式能集成更多信息、更好地保留已有内容，且每生成token新增知识更多。通过KPR修订后的文章，比只结合搜索的最前沿模型更擅长支撑问答，尤其能覆盖仅在其它语言中存在的知识。

Conclusion: KPR框架提升了文档持续更新的效率和可解释性，在多语言场景和知识丰富应用中表现出明显优势，尤其有助于知识全面融合和内容可追溯管理。

Abstract: We introduce Knowledge Pull Requests (KPRs), a framework for continual document authoring that makes each change interpretable. Documents require ongoing revision as new knowledge surfaces from other sources, languages, or times, but existing approaches either edit with no account of what knowledge changed or regenerate from scratch. A KPR integrates new knowledge into a document by extracting claims, filtering and routing them to sections, and flagging conflicts with existing content, producing a ChangeLog that separates what knowledge changes (claim proposal) from how the text changes (document diff). We evaluate KPRs on revising Wikipedia across languages and updating query-driven reports on RAGTIME. KPRs integrate more information and better preserve existing content than rewriting from sources or regenerating from scratch, while adding the most information per token generated. A KPR-revised article also grounds question answering better than a frontier model with search, which does not surface knowledge documented only in other languages.

</details>


### [181] [Capable yet Parsimonious: Extracting and Characterizing Hidden Chain-of-Thought in Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26637)
*Xiaoyu Luo,Tao Ren,Wenrui Yu,Xiao Li,Qiongxiu Li,Johannes Bjerva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过API工具诱导前沿大模型外化中间推理过程，并系统分析其与原生推理的异同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型能力提升多归因于推理能力增强，但闭源模型的推理过程无法直接观测。本研究希望通过诱导模型外化中间推理，验证并分析其推理能力和特点。

Method: 通过注册自定义工具，利用标准API功能，诱导闭源前沿模型（如GPT-6 Astra）在推理过程中外化中间步骤，并与开源模型的原生链式推理进行比较。此外，具体比较了不同模型外化推理时的结构、压缩与组织方式。

Result: 外化推理与原生推理表现一致，均明显优于无推理的基线。在数学、科学和代码任务中，前沿模型展现出高效、结构化的推理能力，Astra模型尤以早期选择正确路径和外化关键步骤见长。

Conclusion: 该方法为理解闭源前沿语言模型的推理机制提供了新视角，揭示了外化推理过程中的系统性差异和高效行为，对模型能力评估具有重要意义。

Abstract: The rapid capability gains of frontier language models are widely attributed to improved reasoning abilities, yet this cannot be verified as raw CoT traces in closed-source systems are hidden. By registering a simple custom tool through a standard API feature, we induce frontier models to externalize intermediate reasoning. Because these traces may reflect post-hoc rationalization rather than genuine reasoning, we first evaluate against native CoT on open-source models and extend to closed-source frontier models including GPT-6 Astra. We find that the extracted reasoning matches native reasoning performance and substantially outperforms no-reasoning baselines, across competition mathematics, science, and code generation. We then characterize how frontier models structure their intermediate reasoning. Across token efficiency, reasoning-step types, and induced reasoning trees, we identify systematic differences in how models externalize, compress, and organize reasoning. We find that Astra exhibits token-efficient directed reasoning, selecting a correct trajectory earlier, while resolving elementary steps internally and externalizing only crucial reasoning. These findings provide a behavioral lens on frontier-model reasoning beyond benchmark scores.

</details>


### [182] [Diffusion Drafts, AR Verifies: Accelerating Document OCR with Self-Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26638)
*Dohyun Kim,Sungjun Han,Hyungguk Kim,Yusik Kim,Jamin Shin,Paul Hongsuck Seo,Hongjoon Ahn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GravityOCR提出了一种结合自回归（AR）和扩散（diffusion）机制的OCR模型，在保证高精度的同时显著提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自回归OCR方法因逐步生成每个token，推理速度慢；而扩散方法虽然并行生成，易引入错误。因此，需要一种新方法兼顾准确性和速度。

Method: 提出GravityOCR模型，将参数共享的自回归块（AR-block）与扩散机制结合，通过并行草稿生成与因果自回归验证联合训练，在每轮中批量提交多个输出token，并用自回归路径进行奖励优化。

Result: 在OmniDocBench v1.6数据集上，AR-path GRPO方案由94.92提升到95.16，保持与GLM-OCR（95.48）相近水平。在部署时，平均每轮提交9.7个token，解码速度提升达3.94倍，端到端页面处理提速1.32倍。

Conclusion: GravityOCR有效提升了OCR推理速度，同时保持了高精度，验证了参数共享和AR验证的实用性，适用于实际部署。

Abstract: Autoregressive OCR vision-language models accurately convert document images into text and structured markup, but require one sequential decoding step per output token, limiting inference speed. Unlike open-ended text generation, OCR outputs are strongly grounded in the input image, making diffusion-based parallel generation promising. However, when several tokens are predicted in one diffusion step, each is predicted before the others are known. Committing them directly can therefore introduce errors. We therefore introduce GravityOCR, a parameter-shared AR-block-diffusion model jointly trained for parallel drafting and causal AR verification. Verifying drafts before commitment lets the model commit multiple output tokens per round without a separate drafting network. The causal AR path also enables GRPO with sequence- and structure-level OCR rewards, avoiding diffusion-trajectory likelihood estimation while updating the shared drafter parameters. On OmniDocBench v1.6, AR-path GRPO improves the Overall score from 94.92 to 95.16 without reducing diffusion drafting efficiency, while the final model remains close to the original GLM-OCR score of 95.48. In an SGLang serving deployment, GravityOCR commits an average of 9.7 output tokens per forward pass and achieves a $3.94\times$ decode-only speedup on region crops and a $1.32\times$ end-to-end page-processing speedup over AR decoding.

</details>


### [183] [Detecting GPT-Assisted Writing Using Interpretable Stylometric Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26687)
*Rajesh Kumar,Nabeel Siddiqui,Alexander Fuchsberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文通过分析文本内部的风格特征，尝试区分学生独立创作与使用GPT辅助写作的文本，取得了较好辨识效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPT等大语言模型在写作中的广泛应用，学界亟需有效方法来识别学生是否借助AI辅助写作，以保证学术诚信。

Method: 作者招募90名参与者，分别完成独立写作和ChatGPT辅助写作任务。使用语法和词汇等可解释性风格特征，测试了八种机器学习分类器，并采用严格的数据划分确保同一参与者数据不混杂。最终使用Random Forest模型进行区分，并利用SHAP分析解释模型表现。

Result: 在留出测试集上，Random Forest模型的ROC-AUC达到0.87，F1分数为0.84，误报率（假阳性）为22.2%，漏报率（假阴性）为11.1%。SHAP分析显示，语法和词汇特征对模型判别贡献最大。

Conclusion: 通过分析文本自身的可解释风格特征，可以较精准地识别出GPT辅助写作，且该特征具备一定透明度与可解释性，为学术诚信检测提供有力工具。

Abstract: Distinguishing GPT-assisted from independently authored student writing has become a critical challenge in academia. This paper evaluates the discriminative capability of interpretable stylometric features extracted solely from submitted text. Using data from 90 participants who wrote both independently and with ChatGPT assistance, we evaluate eight machine learning classifiers while keeping data from the same participant together during validation. On the held-out test set, Random Forest achieved an ROC-AUC of 0.87 and an F1-score of 0.84, with False Positive and False Negative rates of 22.2% and 11.1%, respectively. SHAP analysis shows that lexical and grammatical characteristics drive the resulting predictions. The findings suggest that transparent, text-intrinsic features provide measurable signal for detecting GPT-assisted writing.

</details>


### [184] [Measuring the Serving Stack Instead of the Model: Hidden Confounds in Local Tool-Use Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26693)
*Lijuan Tang,Yuemeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在本地部署AI模型时，调用工具接口的行为不仅仅取决于模型本身，还受部署和服务层实现影响，从而影响被测模型的工具调用准确率评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型与工具对接（tool-call）评估往往只关注模型本身，忽视了提供服务的技术栈（如Ollama、vLLM等）对评测的实际影响，可能产生误判。需要厘清服务层实现如何干扰协议执行与评价。

Method: 作者分析、对比了不同服务框架（Ollama、llama.cpp、vLLM、SGLang）下模型工具调用的解析、拒绝和容错表现，包括不同的协议实现细节、错误处理以及统计方式。

Result: 不同服务架构对同样的模型工具调用请求表现各异。协议差异或实现策略会导致调用错误被错误归因于模型本身，如某些拒绝或重试未被准确记录，进一步统计会出现误报。例如，采用文本形式协议反而降低了支持原生调用协议的模型的表现统计值。

Conclusion: 评测大模型工具调用能力时，必须将服务实现层行为纳入评测流程的规范，否则结果会有系统性偏差。文末给出实际评测须关注的细节清单。

Abstract: A coding agent must emit a valid tool call--a parseable invocation of a tool in the provided schema--before the harness can execute its chosen action. We study how local serving stacks affect this protocol step and show that measured outcomes can depend on the serving layer rather than model behavior alone. In Ollama, the default tools= request is gated per model by a static template flag: some models are accepted and return calls as text, some return native tool_calls, while Phi-3 and Gemma-3 are rejected before inference. In our harness, rejection and retry exhaustion are not preserved as structured failure metadata, so downstream analysis can misclassify them as model non-calls and naively report 0% fidelity. Adding a text tool list while retaining the native channel recovers much of the measured fidelity for accepted models, whereas a uniform text protocol reduces fidelity for Llama-3.2, which has native tool-call support. Cross-stack probes on Ollama, llama.cpp, vLLM, and SGLang show different handling of the same request. Constrained decoding removes parse failures but can induce non-termination, and turn-pooled versus per-instance estimates differ by up to about 55 points. We conclude with a checklist for treating serving behavior as part of the evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Repeated Sampling: Learning Search Policies for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26704)
*Ismail Labiad,Matthieu Kowalski,Marc Schoenauer,Rémi Munos,Julia Kempe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过语义层面的探索策略增强大模型在难推理任务下的表现，如先生成多样化的关键概念，再据此生成答案，显著优于传统的重复采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在难推理任务中依赖单纯重复独立采样，希望通过局部解码噪声获得正确答案，但这种方式探索有限，产出高度重复且缺乏多样性。本文关注能否在语义层面上实现有效探索，提升解题表现。

Method: 提出“双阶段探索”方法：首先抽取多样化的题目相关概念、提示或策略，再以此为条件生成答案。进一步，用小型概念生成器通过强化学习进行训练，使生成的概念能最大化大型（参数冻结的）答案生成器的下游解题成功率。

Result: 在难数学推理任务上，训练后的小型概念生成器能在相同算力预算下显著提高答案生成器的pass@k分数，优于单纯重复采样，还超过了未调优的大型模型生成的概念。同时，该生成器可迁移到从未接触过的答案生成模型上，甚至不同模型家族也有效。

Conclusion: 通过训练小型概念生成器，将其作为大型模型的高效可复用搜索策略，可大幅提升大模型解难题的能力，超越传统采样及静态大模型提示。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly tackle hard reasoning problems by spending more test-time compute, yet the dominant strategy remains naive repeated sampling: draw many independent solutions and hope one is correct. Because such sampling explores only through local decoding noise, it tends to produce many near duplicate attempts rather than genuinely different ideas. We ask whether exploration can instead be steered at a semantic level, by first sampling problem specific concepts, hints, or strategies and then conditioning answer generation on them. We refine this into a simple, more exploratory procedure that emits many diverse concepts in a single trajectory, and evaluate it on hard problems where repeated sampling struggles. We then go a step further and make concept generation trainable: a small concept generator is optimized with reinforcement learning so that its concepts maximize the downstream success of a larger, frozen answer generator. On hard mathematical reasoning problems, the trained concept generator substantially improves the answer generator's pass@k over naive repeated sampling at the same answer generation allocation, surpasses concepts drawn from much larger untuned models, and transfers to answer generators it was never trained against, including a model from a different family. A small model can thus be trained into an effective, reusable search policy for a much larger one.

</details>


### [186] [SpeakerMem-R1: Speaker-Centered Dual-Track Memory for Multi-Party Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26780)
*Haobo Zheng,Tan Tang,Yan Chen,Weijie Wang,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了SpeakerMem-R1系统，通过双轨道记忆机制提升多方对话中长时记忆的归因与信息整合能力，实现更准确的信息检索和状态重建，在多个公开基准测试中达到了当前最优成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在多方对话的长期记忆中，常常无法正确归因信息、整合分散于不同成员和时序的信息，从而影响多方交流中的人物关系、状态追踪等核心任务效果。因而迫切需要新的方法提升对话归因和关系理解能力。

Method: 提出SpeakerMem-R1系统，采用“双轨道记忆”设计。一条轨道保留角色标记的原句信息，另一条整理并存储结构化的个人及群组状态视图。查询时，系统从实体、事件、时间等维度，集成两条轨道的证据。此外，训练过程中引入SpeakerLevenshtein和带角色条件的GRPO以减少归因和更新误差。

Result: 在GroupMemBench、SocialMemBench及EverMemBench等三个数据集上，SpeakerMem-R1分别获得47.9%、69.2%、61.9%的二元准确率。在EverMemBench公开排行榜上取得了62.33%（当前最佳），在LoCoMo测试集上达70.85%。同时，采用强化学习将基础写作模型准确率由57.38%提升至68.20%。消融实验显示“逐字”与“结构化”两轨道、人群视图互为补充。

Conclusion: 通过创新性的双轨道记忆设计，SpeakerMem-R1极大提升了多方长时对话记忆的归因与状态重建能力，在实际多方交流任务和权威基准测试中取得了显著优越的表现。

Abstract: Long-term conversational memory in multi-party settings requires more than retrieving relevant content from long-term conversations: it must distinguish who said what, whom each statement concerns, how individuals perceive one another, what information is shared by the group, and how states change over time. Recent studies on multi-party dialogue benchmarks show that existing general-purpose LLM memory systems tend to lose person and group relations or struggle to integrate clues distributed across members, groups, and time. Together, these issues reveal two core bottlenecks: message attribution and relational understanding in multi-party dialogue, and state reconstruction from interleaved histories. To address both, we propose $\textbf{SpeakerMem-R1}$: its dual-track memory stores speaker-labeled verbatim messages and derived states organized into person-level and group-level views, then combines evidence from both tracks by entity, event, and time at query time. To reduce attribution and update errors during structured memory construction while enabling local deployment, we train Writer-R1 with SpeakerLevenshtein and speaker-conditioned GRPO. On GroupMemBench, SocialMemBench, and EverMemBench, SpeakerMem-R1 achieves binary accuracies of 47.9%, 69.2%, and 61.9%, respectively. On the publicly reported EverMemBench leaderboard from EverMind-AI, we achieves 62.33%, the best reported result among the latest state-of-the-art frameworks. It also achieves 70.85% on all 1,986 LoCoMo questions, which we use as a two-person long-term conversation boundary test. In a controlled evaluation of 305 questions, RL raises the SFT Writer's mean accuracy from 57.38% to 68.20%. We report both binary accuracy and token-F1, and ablations show that the verbatim and structured tracks, as well as person-level and group-level views, are complementary under the standardized evaluation interface.

</details>


### [187] [Agensh: Scaling Organizational Intelligence to 1,024 Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26781)
*Zhihao Zhan,Ting Song,Li Dong,Shaohan Huang,Jianxun Lian,Yan Xia,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了无需中心协调器、支持大规模自组织的多智能体系统Agensh，可提升复杂任务的并发、协作和通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统的扩展性受限于中心协调器的处理能力，难以高效应对任务分配与协作，制约了在大规模、低延迟复杂任务中的应用。

Method: Agensh 通过引入无中央协调器的自组织多智能体协作循环，让各智能体自主认领和分配子任务，异步推进任务进展，并依托于共享工作区、消息接口和共享上下文三大基础设施，实现信息交流和协作。

Result: 在五个 ProgramBench 最难任务上，智能体数量从1扩展到128时，通过率从19.31%提升到28.78%；在pandoc任务上，从1扩展到1024时，通过率由33.89%提升至55.06%。多智能体合作随规模增长变得更为规范有序。

Conclusion: 智能体数量成为多智能体系统扩展的新维度，可用于拓展通用智能的边界，为严格时延或时间预算下的复杂任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A multi-agent system can reduce latency on complex tasks by executing work concurrently. Several pioneering harness frameworks support multi-agent systems. However, the scalability of current multi-agent harnesses is often constrained by a central orchestrator's capacity to allocate tasks and coordinate workers. To address this limitation, we introduce Agensh, a scalable self-organized multi-agent harness without a central orchestrator: concurrent workers execute a multi-agent cooperation loop, continuously gathering context, claiming and self-assigning sub-tasks, taking action and sharing findings, verifying results, and merging progress in an asynchronous manner. The loop is supported by the agentic organization infrastructure comprising three components: a shared workspace holds proposed, ongoing, and completed work; a message interface lets workers communicate; and shared context retains reusable findings and work intentions. To test the scalability of Agensh, we evaluate it on the five hardest ProgramBench tasks with GPT-5.6-sol (high). Scaling from 1 to 128 agents raises the mean final test-pass rate from 19.31% to 28.78%, an approximately 49% relative improvement. Larger organizations reach comparable test-pass rates earlier. On pandoc, scaling from 1 to 1,024 agents raises the final test-pass rate from 33.89% to 55.06%. Worker trajectories further show that different forms of self-organized cooperation gradually emerges and standardizes as the organization grows. These results reveal the number of agents as a new scaling dimension for multi-agent organizations to expand the frontier of general intelligence, offering a practical solution for complex tasks under hard latency constraints or time budgets.

</details>


### [188] [Flash-dLLM: IO-Aware KV Caching and Parallel Decoding for Fast, Memory-Efficient Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26796)
*Quan Nguyen-Tri,Mukul Ranjan,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Flash-dLLM是一种面向扩散式大语言模型（dLLM）的推理加速框架，显著提升了dLLM的推理速度和内存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散式大模型（dLLM）在推理时受限于低效的KV缓存和并行解码方式，尤其是当两者结合时会出现I/O瓶颈，影响实际部署效果。

Method: Flash-dLLM提出了一种I/O感知的融合式KV缓存机制，减少冗余的显存读写，并设计了一种无需辅助模型的KV缓存驱动草拟-验证式解码策略，提升并行解码效率。

Result: 在数学推理（GSM8K）和代码生成（HumanEval）等基准上的实验显示，Flash-dLLM推理速度和内存效率均优于目前最强的Elastic-Cache基线，最高取得5.1倍和11倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: Flash-dLLM极大缓解了dLLM推理中的I/O瓶颈，实现了更高效、更可扩展的解码，为dLLM在实际中的应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive LLMs by enabling non-autoregressive text generation. However, their practical deployment remains limited by inefficient inference, largely due to the absence of effective Key-Value (KV) caching and scalable parallel decoding mechanisms. Existing acceleration methods typically study KV caching and parallel decoding in isolation, overlooking the I/O bottlenecks that arise when cache reuse and parallel token verification are jointly applied. In this work, we introduce $\textbf{Flash-dLLM}$, a training-free inference acceleration framework for fast and memory-efficient dLLMs. Flash-dLLM first identifies GPU memory I/O as a dominant bottleneck in KV-cache-enabled dLLM inference and addresses it with an I/O-aware fused KV-cache kernel that reduces redundant memory movement. Building on this optimized cache mechanism, Flash-dLLM further proposes an efficient KV-cache-driven draft-and-verify decoding strategy, where the dLLM itself serves as both drafter and verifier without requiring an auxiliary model. This unified design enables faster decoding while preserving generation quality and improving scalability to longer sequences and larger batch size. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks demonstrate that Flash-dLLM consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art dLLM acceleration methods in both inference speed and memory efficiency. In particular, it achieves $5.1\times$ and $11.0\times$ speedups over prior strongest baseline Elastic-Cache on GSM8K and HumanEval, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Towards Adaptive Interaction Strategies for Human Companion Robot via Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25031)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的新型人机伴随策略，使机器人能够灵活调整在人类身边的位置，应对实际环境变化，并显著提升伴随任务的成功率、跟踪精度与用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机伴随方法大多局限于固定位置如跟在人后、前或并行，导致机器人在动态环境中适应性差，难以拓展多样实际应用。

Method: 提出利用深度强化学习（DRL）的方法，构建机器人与人的互动空间作为DRL的状态空间，结合模型预测路径积分（MPPI）与控制障碍函数（CBF）开发伴随控制器，实现对位置与朝向精准跟随，并考虑避障与社交接受度。

Result: 在实际室内外场景中进行对比实验，提出方法的成功率和跟踪精度分别提升至少24%和47%，提升了人的舒适感。机器人能灵活伴随移动速度高达1.7 m/s的人，并动态调整策略，规避固定位置限制。

Conclusion: 该方法能提升机器人在人机交互中的灵活性、安全性和舒适感，有助于推动机器人在实际环境中的广泛应用。

Abstract: In the field of Human-Robot Interaction (HRI), achieving flexibility in human-accompanying within real-world environments holds great potential for various applications but also poses significant challenges. Traditional methods typically restrict robots to fixed positions relative to humans, such as tracking from behind, in front, or side-by-side, which limits robot adaptability in dynamic workspaces. This study introduces a novel human-companioning strategy that uses Reinforcement Learning (DRL) to enable mobile robots to dynamically adjust their tracking positions according to varying conditions. An interaction space is defined to capture the relationship between the human and the robot while considering the environment, which serves as the basis for state spaces in DRL to assist the robot in adapting to environmental changes. A human-robot companion controller is developed by integrating Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control with Control Barrier Functions (CBF), ensuring that the robot accurately follows the target's movement in both position and orientation while avoiding obstacles and enhancing social acceptance and safety. The proposed approach is evaluated in real-world scenarios, both indoors and outdoors, and compared with other studies. The results show that the proposed method improves the success rate and tracking accuracy by at least 24% and 47%, respectively, while enhancing human comfort. Experiments demonstrate the robot's ability to flexibly accompany a person walking at speeds of up to 1.7 m/s, dynamically adjusting its strategy without being confined to a fixed position. Additionally, the robot respects the human's intimate space to ensure safety, comfort, and effective obstacle avoidance.

</details>


### [190] [Cosserat Modeling of Trimmed Helicoid Soft Arms with a Separated-Section Constitutive Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25264)
*Zhihang Qin,Linxin Hou,Zeyu Zhong,Yuchen Sun,Wenci Xin,Yueheng Zhang,Ji Qi,Jie Wang,Muhammad Sunny Nazeer,Yu Jun Tan,Federico Renda,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种适用于分离螺旋结构软体机器人的Cosserat杆模型分段刚度计算方法，并显著提高了建模精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Cosserat杆模型假设所有受力材料均位于统一截面，这对于存在稀疏融合和分离螺旋域的软体机器人就不准确。新型螺旋臂结构由于稀疏的材料融合点导致力学性能发生改变，传统方法无法捕捉这一特性，因此需要新的分段本构建模方法。

Method: 本文提出分离域本构定律：在每个螺旋域的局部坐标系下评估材料刚度，并将本构响应映射回主骨架，计算获得等效主骨架刚度。建模时加入稀疏融合机制，量化各材料道之间的相对运动附加柔顺性，推导得到各截面道的弯曲、扭转和伸长刚度的降阶剖面。模型嵌入到严格的动态Cosserat杆模型中，结合GVS离散化与缆驱动。

Result: 分离域机制导致等效截面刚度呈现强各向异性：弯曲和伸长刚度降低约一个数量级，扭转刚度几乎无影响。模型在103组实测数据集的归一化位置误差分别为7.7%、6.7%和7.8%；在Cascade Lake单核CPU上每次全臂计算仅需0.3秒。

Conclusion: 该方法能高效、准确捕捉结构异质、稀疏融合软体机器人的动力学特性，为基于模型的规划、状态与载荷估计以及软体机器人结构-控制协同设计提供有力工具。

Abstract: Cosserat rod models for soft robots usually construct sectional stiffness by summing material properties over a common cross-section. This assumption becomes inaccurate for trimmed helicoid arms, where load-bearing helix domains are separated and connected only through sparse fused crossings. This paper formulates a separated-section constitutive law that evaluates each helix domain in its local frame and pulls its constitutive response back to the backbone, yielding an effective backbone stiffness. Sparse-fusion mechanics captures the additional compliance caused by relative motion between neighboring domains and determines channel-wise reduction profiles $η_c(s/L)$ for bending, torsion, and extension. The resulting effective sectional stiffness is strongly anisotropic: bending and extension are reduced by about one order of magnitude, whereas torsion remains close to the effective backbone stiffness. The resulting sectional law is embedded in a geometrically exact dynamic Cosserat model with GVS discretization and routed-tendon actuation. Across 103 measured configurations, the three datasets give pooled normalized position errors of \SI{7.7}{\percent}, \SI{6.7}{\percent}, and \SI{7.8}{\percent}, while each full-arm solve requires approximately \SI{0.3}{s} on one CPU core (Intel Xeon, Cascade Lake, \SI{2.8}{GHz}), enabling rapid model-based planning, state and load estimation, and morphology--control co-design for architected soft robots.

</details>


### [191] [Beyond the Flat Seafloor: A Closed-Form Two-View Constraint to Aid Sidescan Sonar Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25271)
*Kalin Norman,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多视几何的方法，正式提出了侧扫声纳两视图的几何约束，可减少对海底平坦等简化假设的依赖，提升水下声纳测绘的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的侧扫声纳处理方法多依赖于近似简化（如假设平坦海底），但这种假设常导致精度下降。本文提出更准确的几何建模和约束，减少对不准确假设的依赖。

Method: 论文形式化了侧扫声纳二维视图下的几何约束，并通过数学推导证明：同一特征点在两次观测下受限于球与平面的交线上。还通过蒙特卡洛仿真分析了不同装载平台和几何安装参数对模糊区域大小的影响，并通过仿真生成测绘路径规划建议。

Result: 结果显示：不确定性区域长度与仰角偏差密切相关，在约20度的交叉角度下达到峰值，而在平行或反向通过时不确定性最小。这些发现有助于合理规划声纳测量路径。

Conclusion: 论文突破性地应用了多视几何理论于侧扫声纳观测，对提升侧扫声纳定位和测绘精度具有指导意义，尤其在路径规划上给出具体建议。

Abstract: Sidescan sonar is a common sensor for both manned and autonomous marine exploration and mapping, yet very few methods build upon or exploit the geometric projection model of the sensor. As sidescan sonar is limited to a 1D range measurement, many approximations are frequently used, including the long-standing assumption of a flat seafloor. Rather than make similar approximations, this paper focuses on a multi-view geometry based approach and formalizes a two-view geometric constraint and proves that a shared feature is constrained to a locus within the intersection of a sphere and a plane. In addition, we characterize what governs the size of the ambiguity locus through Monte Carlo simulation that is grounded in real aperture and mounting geometry for both a surface vessel and an underwater vehicle. We translate additional simulations of relative trajectories for both vehicle platforms into concrete survey-planning guidance. Our results show that locus length is strongly governed by elevation misalignment, and peaks at a moderate oblique crossing angle of approximately 20 degrees, with minimal locus lengths obtained at near parallel and anti-parallel passes.

</details>


### [192] [Learning to Plan in Human-Robot Collaboration: Multimodal Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25274)
*Afagh Mehri Shervedani,Siyu Li,Natawut Monaikul,Bahareh Abbasi,Barbara Di Eugenio,Miloš Žefran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了使用强化学习生成机器人多模态交互策略的方法，使机器人能更有效地协助老年人和残障人士。相较于手工设计规程，该方法可扩展且效果良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前为机器人助理设计交互管理器通常需要手工制定，其面对多模态复杂交互时难以扩展和维护。亟需自动化、可扩展的交互策略生成方式。

Method: 作者提出利用强化学习（RL）自动学习多模态交互策略。在模拟器中，通过利用人类数据和简单高层奖励函数对机器人进行训练，强化对语言和动作等多模态信号的处理，并设置训练前置条件以加快收敛速度。

Result: 在现实环境的人体实验中，该系统展现了良好的可用性和任务完成效率，表现优于传统的对话系统规程设计。

Conclusion: 采用强化学习自动生成机器人多模态交互策略，为多模态人机协作场景中的交互管理器设计提供了可扩展、易解释的新方法。

Abstract: Robot assistants for older adults and people with disabilities need to perform collaborative tasks with users effectively. The core component of these systems is an interaction manager whose job is to observe and assess the task and infer the state of the human and their intent for the robot to choose the best course of action. Due to the sparseness of the data in this domain, the policy for such multimodal systems is often crafted by hand; as the complexity of interactions grows, this process is not scalable. This paper proposes a reinforcement learning (RL) approach to automatically generate the multimodal policy of the robot. Our system focuses on a realistic scenario where a robot assists a user in locating objects within a home environment, managing multimodal signals, including language and physical actions, to select the best action. In contrast to traditional dialog systems, our agent is trained with a simulator that uses human data and can deal with multiple modalities. We use a simple high-level reward function that needs no fine-tuning and enforce some preconditions to speed up the training process. A human study evaluating the system in a real-world setting demonstrates promising results, indicating high usability and effective task completion. This RL-based approach offers a scalable and interpretable alternative for designing interaction managers in multimodal human-robot collaborations.

</details>


### [193] [JAMB: Joint Action-Motion Diffusion for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25322)
*Chuyang Xiao,Peilin Meng,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对双手协调操作的新型扩散策略JAMB，能够更有效地处理复杂的几何关系，在仿真和实际机器人任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往方法难以准确模拟双手操作时未来几何变化的影响，现有基于扩散或预测的方法分别存在独立建模、交互信息利用不足等问题。

Method: 提出JAMB扩散策略，同时对操作动作和未来三维点轨迹进行联合去噪，并通过共享Transformer实现信息交互，且利用统一时空坐标促进多模态表示的空间感知。

Result: 在16个仿真任务中，JAMB平均成功率达到83.4%，较最强基线提升23.9个百分点；在3个真实机器人任务中，对比基线分别提升50.0和21.2个百分点，并在复杂和分布外场景下表现更佳。

Conclusion: JAMB在联合动作-运动建模下实现了更强的双手协调操控能力，优于现有基线方法，对实际机器人多任务应用具有广阔前景。

Abstract: Coordinated bimanual manipulation is challenging because the motion of either arm can alter the shared 3D scene and thereby affect the other arm. Yet most diffusion policies generate actions without explicitly modeling these future geometric consequences, while predictive variants typically use future state only as auxiliary supervision or fixed conditioning. We address this limitation by proposing JAMB, a diffusion policy that jointly denoises bimanual actions and future 3D point tracks. By allowing action and track hypotheses to evolve together within a shared Transformer, each can inform and refine the other throughout denoising. We further ground multimodal representations in a shared spatiotemporal coordinate system to facilitate geometry-aware interaction during joint denoising. We evaluate JAMB on diverse bimanual manipulation tasks in RoboTwin 2.0 and on a real-world robot, comparing it with action-only policies and alternative future-prediction approaches spanning different state representations and learning objectives. Across 16 simulation tasks, JAMB achieves an average success rate of 83.4%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 23.9 percentage points. On three real-world tasks, it outperforms the action-only and auxiliary geometry prediction methods by 50.0 and 21.2 percentage points, respectively. Beyond these performance gains, JAMB shows stronger generalization to cluttered scenes and out-of-distribution backgrounds than the evaluated baselines. Together, these results demonstrate the effectiveness of our joint action-motion modeling framework for coordinated bimanual manipulation. Our project website is available at https://jam-bimanual.github.io/

</details>


### [194] [GINIO: A Geometric SO(3)-Equivariant Interface for Neural Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25338)
*Chankyo Kim,Minghan Zhu,Tzu-Yuan Lin,Avantika Rattan,Maani Ghaffari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在IMU任意安装方向下具备SO(3)等变性的神经惯性测程接口GINIO，实现了更几何一致、有效的IMU测量输出，并显著提升了精度和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经惯性测程方法在IMU安装朝向、测量输出的几何一致性等方面存在不足，导致系统的准确性和泛化能力受限，需要一种对任意旋转等变并具备不变性的神经测量方案。

Method: 提出了SO(3)等变的神经IMU测程接口GINIO，能输出在任意旋转下几何变换一致的运动测量及不确定性。同时引入了Last-Frame Alignment（LFA）预处理方法，使得传感器本地训练等价于世界框架下的SO(3)等变训练。系统支持多种滤波与回归结构，系统地提升了滤波、重定向等能力。

Result: 实验表明，GINIO在TLIO数据集上ATE达到2.018 m（而EqNIO退化为76.389 m且其计算量为GINIO的11.6倍）；NanoBench中AirIO-style结构将ATE从5.579 m提升到1.430 m，ResNet-style达到0.581 m优于基线0.645 m；Fetch数据下GINIO无重训练将ATE从8.15 m降到0.50 m。协方差等变性优化误差超过3个数量级优于主对角头。

Conclusion: GINIO实现了对IMU安装方向和物理重定位高度鲁棒的神经惯性测程方法，具备优秀的泛化能力、较低计算量和几何一致性，对实际IMU配准与动态场景有现实应用价值。

Abstract: Neural inertial odometry increasingly uses networks as learned measurements inside filtering pipelines. Such measurements should transform consistently under arbitrary IMU mounting conventions: their mean must transform as a vector, and their covariance must transform congruently as a second-order tensor. We present GINIO, a geometric SO(3)-equivariant interface for neural inertial odometry under arbitrary rotations of the IMU measurement frame. Given calibrated IMU windows, our framework predicts a motion measurement and uncertainty obeying these tensorial laws. To support efficient sensor-frame learning, we introduce Last-Frame Alignment (LFA), a deterministic preprocessing step that is provably equivalent to world-frame training for SO(3)-equivariant predictors. The connected estimator tracks sensor-local states such as IMU bias, separating nuisance estimation from the geometric law enforced by the learned measurement. We instantiate the same interface in filter-connected NIO, AirIO-style recurrent aerial prediction, EqNIO-style full-SO(3) canonicalization, and ResNet-style temporal backbones. On TLIO, GINIO achieves 2.018 m ID/SO(3) ATE while EqNIO degrades to 76.389 m, using 11.6x fewer FLOPs. On NanoBench, our AirIO-style instantiation improves ATE from 5.579 m to 1.430 m without external attitude input, and our ResNet-style instantiation reaches 0.581 m ATE versus 0.645 m for ResNet1D. On Fetch, GINIO empirically reduces unseen physical-remount ATE from 8.15 m to 0.50 m without retraining, demonstrating robustness beyond the exact coordinate-frame guarantee. For uncertainty, spectral covariance reduces covariance-equivariance error by over three orders of magnitude compared with a diagonal head.

</details>


### [195] [Learning from Humans for Proactive Assistance in Human-Robot Collaborative Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25351)
*Elvin Yang,Christoforos Mavrogiannis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机协作搬运框架PROACT，通过预测人类协作行为提升机器人对障碍环境下大物体搬运的响应性和效率，在真实测试中大幅减少用户负担和完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人-机器人协作运输领域，机器人既需对用户输入做出物理响应，又要在搬运重物过程中帮助用户降低劳动强度并提升效率。以往方法难以兼顾顺应性和自主性。本文希望通过结合预测与控制，构建更优秀的人机搬运协作机制。

Method: 作者提出了PROACT框架，引入对人类协作行为的预测模型，将其与合规性的全身机器人控制结合。该模型利用Transformer网络，基于大量现实人类协作搬运数据进行训练，实现对未来物体运动的预测，并据此优化机器人的操作策略。

Result: 在108次真实世界测试中，使用9自由度移动机器人，PROACT相较仅顺从控制和MPC基线方案，分别减少了59.2%/20.4%的平均人机交互做功和12.9%/6.9%的完成时间。

Conclusion: 将人类行为预测与机器人合规控制相结合，能显著提升人机协作搬运的效率和顺应性，对物流、制造与家庭等实际应用具有广泛意义。

Abstract: We focus on human-robot collaborative transport, a challenging task of broad relevance spanning logistics, manufacturing, and the home, in which a user and a robot work together to relocate a large or heavy object. To act as an effective partner, the robot should reduce the user's effort by contributing to efficient relocation of the object while remaining physically responsive to them. Prior work often addresses these capabilities separately, producing robots that may move the object efficiently but resist user input, or accommodate the user but depend on continuous guidance. Our key insight is that obstacle-constrained collaborative transport requires integrating predictions of human collaborative behavior with compliant robot control. To this end, we introduce PROACT, a framework for human-robot collaborative transport that incorporates anticipation into compliant whole-body control through a learned model of human collaborative behavior. Trained on a large-scale, real-world dataset of dyadic human transport demonstrations, our transformer architecture distills collaborative behavior into predictions of future object motion. Across 108 real-world trials with a 9-DoF mobile manipulator, PROACT reduces mean interaction work by 59.2\% and 20.4\%, and mean completion time by 12.9\% and 6.9\%, relative to compliance-only and MPC baselines, respectively. Footage from our experiments can be found at https://youtu.be/qAGvQfVPjbk.

</details>


### [196] [HOTICE: Whole-Body Humanoid Object Transportation in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25363)
*Toan Nguyen,Weiduo Yuan,Siheng Zhao,Yue Wang,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HOTICE的新型类人机器人全身学习框架，用于在拥挤环境中安全搬运各种形状的物体，并在仿真和真实机器人上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在拥挤环境中进行物品搬运时受环境限制，特别是空间狭小、碰撞风险高，难以保证机器人和被搬运物品的安全通行。因此，需要一种能在复杂环境中有效协调全身动作并进行碰撞规避的新方法。

Method: 1）提出类人-物体解耦势场方法，同时为机器人本体与携带物体编码避障引导，实现协作式避障运动；2）设计上下肢解耦的双智能体强化学习架构，通过共享状态和奖励机制保障全身协调；3）采用“专家—通才”蒸馏，将特权教师策略学习融合成单一可部署学生策略，以获得更好的泛化。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真和真实Unitree G1机器人实验中，HOTICE展现出高效、鲁棒的物体搬运能力，不同形状物体与不同场景下皆可成功避障，运动协调性和抗环境变化能力强，在仿真到真实环境迁移的表现也很优异。

Conclusion: HOTICE能够可靠实现类人机器人在狭窄、拥挤环境中的全身和物体结合避障运动，对未来实际服务机器人等场景具有重要意义。

Abstract: Object transportation is a fundamental capability for humanoid robots operating in real-world, human-centric environments, yet existing methods struggle when clutter constrains free space around both the robot and its carried payload. We present HOTICE, a whole-body humanoid learning framework for transporting objects through such cluttered environments. First, we introduce Humanoid-Object Decoupled Potential Fields, which jointly encode collision-avoidance guidance for the robot and the carried object, enabling coordinated, obstacle-aware motion for both. Second, to address the large action space inherent to whole-body loco-manipulation, we design a dual-agent reinforcement learning architecture that decouples upper- and lower-body control while preserving whole-body coordination via shared state observations and rewards. To train a policy that generalizes across diverse cluttered scenes, we further employ a specialist-to-generalist distillation strategy, in which privileged teacher policies are distilled into a single deployable student policy. We evaluate HOTICE in MuJoCo simulation and on a real Unitree G1 humanoid, demonstrating effective and robust object transportation across cluttered scenarios for objects of varying shapes. Our results show that HOTICE reliably coordinates whole-body motion and object-aware collision avoidance, generalizing effectively to previously unseen cluttered environments while achieving strong performance in sim2real deployment.

</details>


### [197] [Capability-Aware Arbitration for Semantic Intent-Based Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25369)
*Zhaoda Du,Michael Bowman,Xiaoli Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合人意图信心与机器人能力信心的共享控制新框架，在复杂任务中显著提升了机器人执行效果与用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享控制方法通常仅根据对人类意图的信心来分配机器人自主权，假设机器人能可靠自主完成任务。但如果机器人自身能力有限，该假设失效时可能导致过度协助（over-helping）、影响整体任务表现。因此有必要综合考虑机器人当前能力来更合理分配自主权。

Method: 框架整体包括两个核心模块：1. 基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）推断人类意图，并输出语义意图信心；2. 基于视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）生成自主动作，并根据动作轨迹的随机性分布和局部不稳定性，在线估计VLA能力信心。采用非线性仲裁策略，通过贝叶斯滤波的方法，将上述两种信心以sigmoid映射方式融合，动态调整机器人与人类的控制权分配。在12位受试者的实验中，设计了含分布内/外的取放和双向堆叠任务，对比多种共享控制基线。

Result: 所提方法在实验中表现优异，任务成功率最高达92%，优于手动遥控（83%）、仅基于意图仲裁（44%）和固定等权重融合（10%）。在易用性和控制分歧方面也均优于其他共享控制方法。

Conclusion: 结合机器人的任务执行能力可以更合理分配控制权，减少过度协助，显著提升共享控制系统性能与体验。

Abstract: Shared control often allocates robot authority based on confidence in inferred human intent, assuming reliable autonomous execution. When this assumption fails, high intent confidence can cause over-helping. We present a capability-aware shared-control framework in which a vision-language model (VLM) infers human intent and provides semantic-intent confidence, while a vision-language-action (VLA) policy generates autonomous actions. VLA capability confidence is estimated online from the dispersion and local instability of stochastic action trajectories. We design a nonlinear arbitration policy that combines Bayesian-filtered semantic-intent confidence with VLA capability confidence through a sigmoid mapping to adapt robot authority. Our evaluation combined VLM/VLA confidence assessment with a study involving 12 participants performing pick-and-place and bidirectional stacking under in-distribution and out-of-distribution conditions. The proposed method achieved the highest task success rate (92%), compared with manual teleoperation (83%), intent-only arbitration (44%), and fixed equal-weight blending (10%). It also achieved higher control friendliness and lower authority-weighted disagreement than both shared-control baselines. These results demonstrate the benefit of incorporating VLA capability into authority allocation to mitigate over-helping and improve shared-control performance.

</details>


### [198] [Angular momentum analysis on Karate roundhouse kicks: a longitudinal case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25374)
*Jan C. L. Lau,Christian Mele,Jonathan Feng-Shun Lin,Katja Mombaur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了空手道回旋踢动作中的角动量（AM）管理，并提出了两种基于AM的新衡量方法，用于分析动作的稳定性和改进过程。结果显示，不稳定踢的总角动量峰值较高，制动峰值较小。新方法有助于辨别部分不稳定原因，但原因多样性导致无法清晰区分。该方法可推广至其他动态运动分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中人体行走及运动常涉及复杂转体，理解其中的力学机制有助于提升人体和机器人、外骨骼的运动稳定性。空手道回旋踢是强烈动态转体的极端案例，研究其角动量管理可为更复杂的人类或机器人运动分析提供理论基础。

Method: 提出两种新的基于角动量的衡量方法，对一位作者长达一年学习空手道回旋踢的数据与一名专家的单次对比数据进行纵向分析。比较稳定与不稳定踢的角动量相关变量，并分析其随时间的变化。

Result: 结果发现：不稳定踢在击打前的总角动量峰值更高，击打后的制动峰值更小。新提出的角动量衡量指标能够识别出部分不稳定踢的原因，但由于不稳定的成因多样，稳定与不稳定踢的界限并不绝对分明。

Conclusion: 角动量衡量新方法可作为动态运动分析工具，但要将其作为稳定性标准，还需结合时间、运动学及压力中心等参数综合考量。该方法具备推广到其他动态动作分析的潜力。

Abstract: Human gait in reality extends beyond straight-line walking, with some situations even requiring drastic back-and-forth rotations. A smooth and stable execution may seem intuitive, but the underlying mechanics remains unknown. The Karate roundhouse kick may be an extreme case of exhibiting dynamic back-and-forth rotations, but investigating the angular momentum (AM) management can potentially inform stability analysis in dynamic human motions and smoother gait in robots and exoskeletons. This paper introduces two new AM-based measures and analyzes AM-related variables to study the target-less retractable back-leg Karate roundhouse kick. The purpose is to understand the underlying AM management, analyze the differences between stable and unstable kicks, and investigate how these variables and measures change over time with improvement. A one-year longitudinal study was conducted with the first author as a Karate student, and a Karate instructor was also recruited for one session as an expert, whose data is used for comparison. Results show that unstable kicks have higher peak total AM before Strike and smaller braking peak after Strike. The proposed AM-based measures are able to identify the cause of some unstable kicks, though no clear distinction could be made between stable and unstable kicks since kicks can be unstable for different reasons. Nonetheless, the proposed measures can be applied as performance measures to analyze other types of dynamic motions. To incorporate them as stability criteria, however, it is recommended to also consider their coordination with time, kinematics, and center of pressure.

</details>


### [199] [PARTE: Plane-Assisted Robust Transformation Estimation for Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25375)
*Abolfazl Babanazari,Carson Cramer,Tyler Summers,Carlos Nieto,Kaveh Fathian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PARTE是一种新颖的点云全局配准方法，能够有效应对重叠度低、结构重复和噪声下的异常点，特别是充分利用平面信息，提高配准鲁棒性和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统点云配准在面对平面区域时效果不佳，常常因特征描述器不足而丢弃平面信息，导致难以处理低重叠和高噪声环境。

Method: 方法提取平面块，并用新型Plane Context Histogram（PCH）对其周边几何进行描述。两级策略筛选平面对应关系，将点与平面对应关系构建兼容性图，结合置信度进行异常值剔除，最后联合进行刚体变换估计。如无合适平面信息，方法会自动退化为仅基于点的配准。

Result: 在6组室内外基准（共8097对）上与13种先进方法对比，PARTE在配准成功率和鲁棒性方面均表现最优，且计算速度较快。

Conclusion: PARTE有效融合点与平面信息，显著提升了各种场景下点云配准的准确性与鲁棒性，提供了适用于不同数据密度和类型的实用方案，并已开源实现。

Abstract: Global point-cloud registration remains challenging when limited overlap, repetitive geometry, and sensor noise produce correspondence sets dominated by outliers. Planar regions are particularly difficult for conventional point descriptors and are therefore often suppressed or discarded before matching. We present PARTE (Plane-Assisted Robust Transformation Estimation), a global registration method that instead treats planar structure as complementary registration evidence. PARTE extracts planar patches and represents them using our novel Plane Context Histogram (PCH), a descriptor that encodes the geometry surrounding each patch, while a two-level matching procedure identifies reliable plane correspondences. Candidate point and plane correspondences are combined in a confidence-weighted compatibility graph for joint outlier rejection, followed by rigid transformation estimation. When no usable plane correspondences are available, PARTE naturally reduces to point-only registration. We evaluate PARTE on 8,097 registration pairs across six indoor and outdoor benchmarks spanning dense RGB-D and sparse LiDAR measurements. Evaluations show PARTE achieves the highest overall success rate against 13 standard and state-of-the-art methods while maintaining low runtime. An open-source C++ implementation with Python bindings is provided at https://parte.pages.dev.

</details>


### [200] [VLAQuantBench: Closed-Loop Evaluation of Post-Training Quantization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25376)
*Jiuyi Xu,Qing Jin,Meida Chen,Song Wang,Yang Sui,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLAQuantBench系统性评估了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的量化策略，多实验揭示配方选择对量化成效有关键影响，提出定制化精度分配优于通用层敏感性规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化可极大地降低大模型存储和部署成本，但VLA模型不同层、数值格式、校准方式交互复杂，当前缺乏细致评测和具体量化配置指引，容易导致显著性能下降。

Method: 提出VLAQuantBench基准，对4个VLA模型在LIBERO等多基准集，共409组实验，94574次模拟，详细测试不同精度、校准、数值格式和量化范围对性能影响。并比较无校准、不同校准与个性化量化设置。

Result: 无校准全模型W4A4量化，扩展部分头部量化范围成功率从7%增至70%；两次回放校准可消除大部分任务失败，部分模型用保护输出层和指定层不量化可达近原始精度；未观察到统一的全局层敏感规律。

Conclusion: VLA量化涉及时层取舍和校准强相关，最佳方案需根据模型与任务定制，不存在一刀切规则。研究为VLA模型量化配置提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Post-training quantization reduces the memory requirements of vision-language-action (VLA) models, but precision selection must account for the interaction between layer scope, numerical format, and calibration. We introduce \textbf{VLAQuantBench}, a controlled evaluation with 409 runs and 94,574 simulation episodes: four models on LIBERO, with X-VLA additionally evaluated on three simulation benchmark families. Under uncalibrated W4A4 round-to-nearest quantization, expanding a $π_{0.5}$ action-head subset from 126 to 167 layers raises success from 7.0\% to 70.5\%. Fixed-observation replay confirms a corresponding numerical recovery. Two-episode calibration removes the severe joint failures in the tested subsets, whereas the same smoothing-and-clipping recipe lowers $π_0$ success and does not recover OpenVLA-OFT end-to-end. For OpenVLA-OFT, protecting one 28,672-parameter output projection instead restores near-baseline success: the remaining 441 eligible linear layers retain W3 on LIBERO-Long or eight-bit activations across all four suites. Task-clustered intervals support the large failure and recovery contrasts. These results establish recipe-dependent interactions and identify concrete precision assignments, rather than universal layer-sensitivity rules. Real-kernel and physical-robot measurements complement the accuracy analysis. Code, configurations, and episode records are publicly available at https://github.com/jiuyixu25/VLAQuantBench.

</details>


### [201] [Norm2Tex: Augmenting Visuo-Tactile Simulations with Texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25398)
*Seongjin Bien,Débora Oliveira Makowski,Roberto Calandra,Florian Walter,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为Norm2Tex的方法，用于增强触觉模拟器的质感细节，提升仿真到真实世界的转移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉模拟器无法精细还原真实世界中的纹理细节，导致仿真数据和真实数据之间有较大的领域差异，影响下游任务的性能。

Method: Norm2Tex通过在渲染流程前修改目标对象的深度图，将法线贴图中的高频表面细节整合进触觉模拟器，实现纹理信息的增强，且可作为插件无缝集成到不同触觉模拟平台中。

Result: 采用Norm2Tex方法后，仿真生成的触觉数据在材质分类和强化学习控制任务上都表现出更优的sim-to-real迁移能力，提升了纹理识别率和材料依赖的控制表现。

Conclusion: Norm2Tex有效保留了跨领域的材质相关触觉信息，促进了从仿真到真实机器人的质感感知和控制能力提升，为机器人触觉感知提供了更为高效的数据生成和迁移手段。

Abstract: Large-scale datasets are essential for training generalist robot control policies. Collecting real-world tactile data is costly and time-consuming, motivating the use of tactile simulations. However, current tactile simulators capture only overall contact geometry and miss fine details like texture. This results in a significant domain shift between simulated and real tactile data. To address this gap, we introduce Norm2Tex, a plug-in method that augments simulations of vision-based tactile sensors with high-frequency surface details from normal map textures. By modifying the target object's depth map before a tactile simulator's rendering pipeline, Norm2Tex seamlessly integrates into different tactile simulators. We also evaluate sim-to-real transfer using material classification and a reinforcement learning task. Our results show that Norm2Tex preserves material-dependent tactile information across domains, improving texture recognition and producing material-dependent control behavior in the real world.

</details>


### [202] [Effects of Assistance Delay on Joint Mechanics and Energetics in Biological Torque Control of a Hip Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25417)
*Jimin An,Ryan Lee,Jingshu Peng,Eni Halilaj,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了不同延迟条件下髋关节外骨骼的助力效果，发现延迟会影响机械卸载但对代谢率影响较小，并指出单一力矩映射策略或难以普适于所有类型步态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物力矩控制策略直接将估算的人体关节力矩映射为外骨骼助力，被认为是支持多样步态的通用方法。但尚不清楚这种固定映射方式对于不同生物力学特征的步态任务效果是否一致。

Method: 招募8名受试者，分别在零力矩和5种不同延迟（40-320毫秒）的主动助力下，完成平地、上坡和下坡三种步行任务，并监测代谢率和关节功指标。

Result: 不论任务和延迟，助力均显著降低了净代谢率和髋关节正功及下肢总正功。延迟对机械输出有负面影响，但对代谢率没有显著影响。不同步态的净代谢率降低幅度不同，下坡步态代谢甚至略有升高。

Conclusion: 生物力矩映射策略的效果需结合关节机械作用和目标效果来评估，主要输出正功的助力方式可能难以推广至以负功为主的步态场景，需做相应调整。

Abstract: Biological torque control directly maps an estimated human joint moment to exoskeleton assistance, providing a task-agnostic strategy for supporting diverse locomotor activities. However, it remains unclear whether a fixed state-to-torque mapping provides effective assistance across biomechanically distinct tasks. We examined how assistance delay affected hip exoskeleton performance during level-ground (LG), ramp-ascent (RA), and ramp-descent (RD) walking. Eight participants completed a zero-torque baseline condition and five active assistance conditions with delays ranging from 40 to 320 ms. Across tasks and active delays, assistance reduced net metabolic rate by 5.24%, positive biological hip joint work by 5.86%, and total lower-limb positive joint work by 1.68% (all p < 0.05). Assistance delay affected both joint-work outcomes (both p < 0.001) but not net metabolic rate. Mechanical unloading generally decreased with increasing delay, whereas metabolic benefits remained comparatively stable. Relative to the zero-torque condition, net metabolic rate decreased by 9.75% during LG and 7.20% during RA but increased by 1.23% during RD. We did not detect task-dependent differences in the delay response. Our findings indicate that biological torque mappings should be evaluated based on the target outcome and mechanical role of the assisted joint, and that predominantly positive-power assistance may not generalize to negative-work-dominant locomotion without modification.

</details>


### [203] [REDACT: Robust Perceptive Locomotion under Unseen Visual Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25450)
*Natapat Kirdwichai,Tobias Driskell-Poole,Andrei Sontea,Jadu Dash,Muhammad Burhan Hafez,Danesh Tarapore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为REDACT的深度感知运动策略，在未知干扰下能更好地保持鲁棒性，实现更高成功率并有良好的零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度的运动策略容易在测试时遇到训练分布外观测（如遮挡、无效返回、噪声、视觉干扰），导致动作不可控，而现有的传感器数据增强方法不能涵盖所有可能的干扰类型。

Method: 提出了REDACT框架，采用改进的视觉编码器、持续特征掩蔽及新颖的共识门控算法，结合近似共形校准，无需先验腐蚀知识来筛选和保留有用深度信息。此外，只对纯净观测数据训练。

Result: REDACT在模拟环境中面对未见过的干扰时，比现有敏捷运动基线有更高的通过率。在不同干扰条件下的深度增强实验也表明，该方法补足了数据增强覆盖不足的短板。

Conclusion: REDACT能显著提升模型对未见深度干扰的鲁棒性，并可零样本迁移至结构化及森林等现实复杂环境，适用于真实场景下的自主运动任务。

Abstract: Depth-conditioned locomotion policies have demonstrated impressive agile maneuvers, but can be steered to unpredictable actions when observations are outside their training distribution. Occlusion, invalid returns, sensor noise, and visual distractors can shift deployment observations away from nominal simulated depth. While synthetic sensor augmentation targets specified degradations, it does not by itself define behavior under corruption families omitted from training. To address gaps in training-time coverage, we present REDACT (Retaining Evidence Despite Artifacts for Continued Traversal), a teacher-student framework combining an improved visual encoder architecture, persistent feature masking, and a novel consensus-gating algorithm to retain useful depth information under unmodeled corruption. The gate uses approximate conformal calibration on clean observations alone, requiring no prior knowledge of the corruption type. Trained on clean simulated depth, REDACT retains useful visual information under unseen corruption, supporting higher traversal success than existing parkour baselines. Evaluation of depth augmentation across corruption families further shows that REDACT improves robustness where augmentation coverage is missing. Real-world trials demonstrate zero-shot transfer to structured and forested environments with unfamiliar scene content.

</details>


### [204] [A bioinspired internal model-based online estimator for planar pursuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25470)
*Tengyue Liu,Xincheng Li,Sofia Morales Ferreira,Kevin Galloway,Udit Halder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种生物启发的内部模型估计算法，可以仅凭部分传感器观测和已知自运动重建交互代理间的相对状态。方法通过优化和约束，将生物运动控制反馈模型应用于机器人，实现实时性能并优于传统状态估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中用于生物启发的追踪、跟随与群体运动控制通常依赖相对状态信息，但实际自主系统往往仅能观测到部分信息（如自身运动），其他关键量无法直接测量。因此需要通过估计方法还原完整的相对状态，实现更智能的控制。

Method: 作者将状态重建建模为一个优化问题：将相对运动学作为约束，通过最小化内部模型输出与传感器观测的差异估计未知状态。理论上应用Pontryagin极大值原理导出最优性条件，并采用前向-后向算法进行数值求解，同时采用滑动窗口实现在线估计。

Result: 所提估计算法在数值仿真中与传统经典状态估计算法对比表现更优，另外在真实机器人硬件系统上基于两种追踪策略实现了该估算框架的实时运行，展现出良好性能。

Conclusion: 生物启发的内部模型估计算法可有效利用部分观测和自运动信息重建系统间的相对状态，为实际智能控制系统提供了理论模型和真实可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Bioinspired feedback controls for pursuit, tracking, and collective motion are often expressed in terms of the relative configuration between interacting agents. In practice, however, onboard sensors may not directly provide all quantities required for feedback control, necessitating estimation of unobserved quantities. This paper develops a bioinspired internal model-based estimator for reconstructing those quantities from partial sensory observations and known self-motion. State reconstruction is posed as an optimization problem that treats the relative kinematics as constraints and minimizes the disagreement between the internal model outputs and measurements from onboard sensors. Pontryagin's Maximum Principle is used to derive the necessary optimality conditions. A forward-backward algorithm is used to provide a numerical solution and a moving horizon formulation is employed for online implementation. The estimator is evaluated numerically against classical state estimators. Real-time implementation of the proposed framework on robotic hardware is demonstrated through two pursuit strategies.

</details>


### [205] [Brace Yourself: Task-Conditioned Environmental Bracing for Forceful Humanoid Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25486)
*Zongyuan Zhang,Christopher Lehnert,Will N. Browne,Jonathan M. Roberts*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持手策略（SHS），使类人机器人能用一只手支撑自身，另一只手进行强力操作，从而提升了机器人操作时的力输出能力和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人在进行需要较大力气的操作时，常因相互作用力影响身体平衡，限制了其在实际场景中的应用能力。因此需要新的方法提高其力操作的能力和安全性。

Method: 通过提出SHS，使机器人一只手与环境支撑，另一只手执行操作，并采用任务条件下的支持配置，引导两个同步强化学习策略，无需人工运动数据或在线全身轨迹规划。

Result: 在Unitree G1机器人上的实验表明，采用SHS后，机器人可产生高达60 N的可用接触力，明显高于未支撑情况下的13.5 N，同时在力跟踪上优于任务无关的支撑配置。该策略还能无须重新训练地适应不同任务区域。

Conclusion: SHS为提升类人机器人强力操作能力提供了一种简单有效的方法，大幅扩展了其操作性能和适应性。

Abstract: Forceful manipulation is challenging for humanoid robots because interaction forces can disturb whole-body balance. We introduce the Supporting Hand Strategy (SHS), which enables a humanoid to brace against the environment with one hand while performing forceful manipulation with the other. SHS optimises a task-conditioned support configuration that guides two synchronous reinforcement-learning policies, without human motion data or online whole-body trajectory planning. On a Unitree G1, SHS achieved usable contact forces up to 60 N, compared with a maximum sustained force of 13.5 N without environmental bracing, while substantially improving force tracking over a task-independent support configuration. The same policies generalised to different task regions without retraining. SHS therefore provides a simple mechanism for substantially extending humanoid forceful-manipulation capability.

</details>


### [206] [RoboMP-DINOv2: Prompts, Not Filters for Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25506)
*Han Qi,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型视觉编码器RoboMP-DINOv2，通过将掩码作为空间提示而非过滤器来增强机器人操作任务的泛化能力和场景理解能力，同时提升了对视觉变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用视觉编码器不适合机器人操作控制，而目标中心方法通过掩码过滤常常丢失了对操作有用的场景上下文信息。如何既保留场景相关信息又提升视觉泛化能力成为亟需解决的问题。

Method: RoboMP-DINOv2对整幅图像提取DINOv2密集特征，并在掩码区域注入学习得到的区域特定嵌入，将掩码作为空间提示参与全局特征融合，实现精准的动作预测。同时提出了掩码区域颜色随机化（MCR）来增强外观鲁棒性，形成RoboMP-DINOv2-MCR。

Result: 在七个模拟操作环境中，RoboMP-DINOv2在空间变化和场景杂乱下的成功率分别为60.7%和59.7%，远优于基于DINOv2的扩散策略（50.7%、41.0%）。在未见颜色物体实验中，RoboMP-DINOv2-MCR成功率达72.5%，而最强基线仅为35.1%。

Conclusion: RoboMP-DINOv2及其MCR扩展显著提升了机器人操作对场景、外观变化的鲁棒性，同时保留了与行为相关的场景信息，对机器人视觉控制领域具有重要意义。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies must generalize across visual shifts while preserving scene context relevant to action. General-purpose vision encoders are not tailored to visuomotor control, while object-centric approaches often use segmentation masks as hard filters that discard potentially useful context. We propose RoboMP-DINOv2 (Robotics Mask-Prompted DINOv2), a full-scene vision encoder that treats masks as spatial prompts rather than visibility filters. It extracts dense DINOv2 features from the full observation, injects learned region-specific embeddings at masked locations, and jointly contextualizes prompted and unprompted tokens for action prediction. We further introduce masked-region color randomization (MCR) to improve appearance robustness, yielding RoboMP-DINOv2-MCR. Across seven simulated manipulation settings, RoboMP-DINOv2 achieves 60.7% success under spatial shifts and 59.7% under scene clutter, compared with 50.7% and 41.0% for a DINOv2-based Diffusion Policy. Under unseen object colors, RoboMP-DINOv2-MCR achieves 72.5% success versus 35.1% for the strongest color-randomized baseline. Additional experiments and representation analyses show improved robustness while preserving behaviorally relevant scene information. Code is available at https://github.com/han20192019/RoboMP_DINOv2.

</details>


### [207] [A Deployment Study of Identity-Gated Drone Gesture Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25511)
*Diyari Mohammed Salih,Ilyes Chaabeni,Naima Ait Oufroukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种身份验证的手势控制系统IGate，结合了人脸识别与手势识别，仅允许授权用户操作无人机，并对其各模块在实验中进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的手势控制系统难以区分用户，任何进入摄像头视野的人都可以下达指令，在共享空间存在安全隐患。为此需要加入身份验证机制。

Method: IGate系统集成人脸识别与跟踪（通过余弦相似度验证当前面部与注册模板是否匹配）和手势识别（采用RBF-SVM分类器，基于手部关键点，使用自建数据集），并以分层有限状态机管理模式切换和异常。人脸注册仅需20帧，免去用户特定预训练。

Result: 在DJI Tello EDU无人机上离线和实际飞行进行了270次测试。人脸验证离线等错误率为0.32%，飞行时为19.3%；RBF-SVM手势识别精度优于传统几何规则方法（0.850对0.651），其中82%的优势得益于深度通道信息。

Conclusion: IGate显著提升了基于视觉手势控制的安全性和可靠性，尤其在多用户或共享环境下。该系统相关数据和脚本将公开，有利于相关研究和应用。

Abstract: Vision-based gesture control accepts commands from any hand in the camera field of view, which is unsafe in shared indoor spaces. This paper presents IGate, an identity-gated control stack that includes gesture control and face tracking, in which commands are admitted only when an enrolled operator is verified. The system performs few-shot user enrolment from 20 initial face frames, without prior user-specific training: verification compares an embedding of the current face crop against the enrolled template by cosine similarity, while face tracking uses proportional correction. Gesture control is achieved by classifying extracted hand landmarks using an RBF-SVM trained on a custom dataset. Additionally, a hierarchical finite-state machine handles mode selection, default, and fallback behaviours. The approach is tested on a DJI Tello EDU, each component evaluated offline and in-flight across 270 trials (149 flown). Face verification yields a 0.32% offline equal error rate versus 19.3% in-flight. Under hover-locked conditions, the RBF-SVM gesture classifier outperforms the geometric rule (0.850 vs. 0.651 accuracy), with 82% of this gap stemming from the depth channel. All logs and reproduction scripts will be released.

</details>


### [208] [Digital Twin-Driven VR Teleoperation with Multi-View Spatial Perception for Surgical Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25527)
*Chang Liu,Chenhao Yu,Honghao Zhao,Hao Ding,Haochen Wei,Adnan Munawar,Mathias Unberath,Peter Kazanzides*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于数字孪生的虚拟现实遥操作平台，实现了通过HMD展示多视角的手术环境并支持自然深度感知，显著提升了手术遥操作体验和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RMIS手术平台多采用固定式控制台和立体内窥镜图像，手术人员视角有限且深度感知受限，同时多数采用手动手势跟踪存在稳定性与精确度问题。提升手术视角自由度和控制精确性成为刚需。

Method: 作者提出结合数字孪生的VR系统，通过无标记感知重建手术环境并实时流式传输至HMD，实现多视角渲染和自然的运动视差。同时使用VR手柄替代手势跟踪，提升操作空间及系统稳定性。

Result: 在15名受试者的da Vinci手术平台实验中，所提VR系统在任务路径长度与平稳性（jerk）上分别比HoloLens 2混合现实基线方案减少86%与95%，并且在深度感知信心上达到或超过传统控制台。

Conclusion: 数字孪生驱动的VR遥操作平台显著提升手术遥操作控制精度、操作自由度和深度感知体验，有望成为新一代便携手术遥操作系统的有效方案。

Abstract: Current robot-assisted minimally-invasive surgery (RMIS) platforms provide a fixed console for the surgeon to view stereo endoscopic images and teleoperate instruments inside the patient. Several researchers have proposed the use of a head-mounted display (HMD) as a portable console, with video pass-through rendering of the endoscope images which, like the fixed console, restricts the operator to a single endoscopic viewpoint and limits depth perception. We present a digital twin-driven virtual reality (VR) teleoperation platform, where the digital twin is created from markerless perception of the surgical environment and streamed for display on the HMD. This overcomes the limitations of video pass-through by providing multi-view rendering and natural motion-parallax cues, enabling decoupling of the user's hand posture from strict instrument alignment. The system utilizes VR hand controllers to increase the teleoperation workspace and to improve the robustness and stability of instrument control compared to the hand tracking approach adopted by most prior systems. A 15-participant user study on the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) shows that our VR platform significantly outperforms a state-of-the-art HoloLens 2 mixed reality baseline, reducing path length by 86% and jerk by 95%, while achieving depth perception confidence comparable to or exceeding the traditional console across all conditions.

</details>


### [209] [HABILIS Brain 0: Geometry-Change Supervision for Vision-Language-Action and Residual Flow Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25558)
*Jinu Pahk,Jesoon Kang,Taegeon Park,Jisu An,Soo Min Kimm,Jaejoon Kim,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Geometry-Change VLA（GC-VLA）的视觉-语言-动作策略，通过多阶段训练利用几何变化监督，显著提升了机器人操作任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作策略虽然借助了几何信息，但仅依赖当前帧无法显式描述操作过程中的动态变化。作者希望研发一种兼容不同实体（机器人或人视角）的视觉交互模型，实现更广泛的预训练和更强的泛化能力。

Method: 方法分为四个阶段：1）用离线帧对训练几何变化预测视觉-语言模型（GC-VLM）；2）引入ActionExpert将视觉-语言模型与具体机器人动作对齐，并阻断梯度流；3）放开梯度流，实现GC-VLM和ActionExpert的联合优化；4）冻结GC-VLA，引入几何调节残差流（GCRF）策略实现闭环控制。模型仅用当前观测和学习到的几何变化表征进行推理。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，GC-VLA单独取得95.20%的任务成功率，加入GCRF后提升到99.55%。

Conclusion: 通过引入多视角未来-当前几何变化作为监督信号，提出的GC-VLA方法极大加强了视觉-语言-动作策略的泛化和操作能力，为跨机器人预训练和灵活适配新任务提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies benefit from geometric supervision, but current-frame geometry alone does not explicitly describe the changes associated with manipulation. This design is motivated by the goal of learning an embodiment-agnostic visual interface that can be pretrained across robot and egocentric video before robot-specific action alignment. We introduce Geometry-Change VLA (GC-VLA), which learns to predict multiview future-current geometry-change tokens from current observations. Offline frame pairs define a nominal 0.5-second prediction horizon; future observations are used only to construct training targets. Stage 1 trains a geometry-change vision-language model (GC-VLM). Stage 2 introduces a continuous ActionExpert and aligns it with robot actions while stopping action-flow gradients at the VLM interface. Stage 3 enables these gradients to update the trainable VLM components jointly with the ActionExpert. Stage 4 freezes GC-VLA and applies Geometry-Conditioned Residual Flow (GCRF), using a binary intervention router and a single bounded residual velocity policy learned from closed-loop feedback. GC-VLA achieves 95.20% success on LIBERO, and GC-VLA with GCRF achieves 99.55%. Inference uses current observations and the learned GC representation without executing the offline target encoders.

</details>


### [210] [IndustrialVLA-Bench: A Traceable Multi-Axis Evaluation of Open Robot Policy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25562)
*Yiqi Wang,Zhifeng Rao,Jiaqi Zhang,Xiaoyang Li,Zhangkai Wu,Yiqun Duan,Mingkai Zheng,Fei Wang,Shan You,Taotao Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了IndustrialVLA-Bench基准，用于统一评测主流视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）与世界-动作模型（WAM）在机器人操控任务中的能力、鲁棒性、语言敏感性及推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA和WAM是开放机器人策略的主流，但两者通常用不同评测协议，导致难以直接对比它们在任务执行、鲁棒性和部署成本等实际指标上的表现。缺少统一、透明、可复现的比较方法。

Method: 构建IndustrialVLA-Bench，选取六个公开VLA和WAM系统；在LIBERO标准任务、LIBERO-Plus鲁棒性测试、LIBERO-Para语言指令变换测试中，统一评测模型能力和鲁棒性；统计推理延迟、内存等部署成本；区分“协议忠实”、“近似复现”、“待验证”等条目，强调严格对比基于协议忠实结果。

Result: 六个系统在标准任务(LIBERO)平均成绩差异仅1.58分，而鲁棒性和指令敏感性成绩跨度更大（分别14.62和31.08分）；即便仅对协议忠实系统考察，差异依然存在。论文还报告每系统的性能消耗、模式和可追溯性证据。

Conclusion: 与其争论哪一范式绝对优越，工业VLA-Bench为两种机器人策略在共同实际指标下的对比提供了透明、严谨的证据基础，对后续算法评测和实际部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: Open robot policies increasingly follow two paradigms: vision-language-action models (VLAs) directly map observations and instructions to actions, whereas world-action models (WAMs) incorporate learned video or world dynamics into policy learning or action generation. Although both target the same manipulation tasks and represent alternative design choices, they are commonly reported under different evaluation protocols, leaving their capability, robustness, language sensitivity, and deployment-cost trade-offs unclear. We present IndustrialVLA-Bench, an evidence-aware evaluation of six released VLA and WAM systems under a unified reporting schema. It separately evaluates clean capability on LIBERO, non-language robustness on LIBERO-Plus, instruction sensitivity on LIBERO-Para, and observed execution cost. Reported task scores aggregate three complete evaluations with distinct random seeds under a fixed checkpoint and inference configuration. Across all six systems, clean LIBERO averages differ by only 1.58 points, whereas robustness and paraphrase summaries span 14.62 and 31.08 points. Restricting every comparison to the three protocol-faithful systems preserves the effect (1.36, 14.62 and 23.10 points), so the diagnostic separation reported here does not depend on the weaker evidence tiers. We additionally report observed inference latency, peak memory, runtime mode, and an evidence status for every system. Protocol-faithful, near-reproduction, and pending-verification entries remain visibly separated; only protocol-faithful entries support strict comparisons. Rather than claiming universal superiority of either paradigm, IndustrialVLA-Bench provides traceable evidence for comparing released robot policies on shared practical criteria. Code and evaluation records are available at https://github.com/xiaoqi-7/IndustrialVLA-Bench.

</details>


### [211] [Recording Hand-Held Laparoscopic Instrument Motion in the Operating Room: Magnetometer-Free Fusion of Inertial, Range and Visual Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25577)
*Jiyul Lee,Dongho Yee,Juahn Oh,Jinseok Lee,Yechan Seo,Seong Jeong,Minsung Kim,Seonho Shim,Younghoon Noh,Hyuk Choi,Youngbin Kong,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种可夹持在常规腹腔镜器械上的运动状态记录器，实现了对手术器械运动的高精度记录，无需磁场参考，显著提升了姿态和位移测量的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前腹腔镜手术中，除了视频信息，器械运动数据并未被保留，而运动轨迹对外科技能评估和机器人学习非常重要。现有基于视频或IMU的方法精度有限，且IMU中的磁力计易受干扰，影响准确性。

Method: 作者设计了一个夹持在手术器械上的状态记录器，不影响进入患者体内的部分。该装置融合了六轴IMU、飞行时间（ToF）测距仪和无标记相机，并在穿刺套管的远心点下通过误差状态卡尔曼滤波进行数据融合。使用U-Net分割器械轮廓以获得方位信息，避免了磁力计的误差。

Result: 旋转角度误差降至0.200°（相比IMU常规误差3.58°），与高精度机械臂对比，平移误差为1.21mm RMS，旋转误差为0.34°RMS；实际连续轨迹绝对末端误差可达1.22mm（程序操控）及3.04mm（远程操控），且融合多传感器后精度最高。

Conclusion: 该装置实现了无需磁场参考的高精度器械运动记录，能实时显示手术器械的深度和姿态，为腹腔镜技术分析、技能评估及机器人学习提供了有价值的数据支持。

Abstract: Most minimally invasive procedures are still performed with hand-held laparoscopic instruments, yet only the endoscopic video is retained; the instrument motion that expresses surgical skill, and that could support skill assessment and robot learning, is lost. Pose from video alone remains millimeters to centimeters off, and an instrument-mounted inertial measurement unit (IMU) cannot rely on its magnetometer, whose field changed with tool pose and between sessions in our measurements. We present a surgical instrument-state logger that clips onto a conventional instrument without modifying the part that enters the patient and fuses a six-axis IMU and a time-of-flight (ToF) rangefinder with a markerless camera in an error-state Kalman filter under the remote center of motion (RCM) of the trocar. Heading comes from the shaft silhouette, segmented by a U-Net, in place of the magnetometer: the rotation-angle error is 0.200°, against 3.58° from the accelerometer and magnetometer alone. Against a Franka Research 3 manipulator, and without alignment to it, the displacement error over 300 translation trials was 1.21mm RMS and the relative-rotation error over 180 rotation trials 0.34° RMS. On continuous trajectories, tracked and displayed in real time, the absolute tip error was 1.22mm (programmed) and 3.04mm (teleoperated) after post-hoc tuning of three filter parameters, and the full fusion beat every sensor subset. Because the estimator uses no magnetic measurement, its accuracy does not rely on an undisturbed field. The same clip-on device could thus record metric tip trajectories during routine hand-held laparoscopy, while displaying the insertion depth and attitude that are hidden once the instrument is inside the patient.

</details>


### [212] [CableVLA: Simulation-Privileged Global-Local Representation Learning for Cable Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25606)
*Zhifei Teng,Bo Feng,Xiang Zou,Jinpeng Xiao,Min Li,Zhouping Yin,Yiqun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CableVLA是一个端到端多模态视觉-语言-动作框架，能够有效地进行电缆布线任务，在仿真和实际环境下均取得了显著成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电缆布线需要同时处理全局拓扑控制和复杂的局部接触问题，传统方法在多模态感知和迁移到真实环境方面存在局限。作者希望开发既能利用仿真优势又能适应真实世界的统一感知-动作系统。

Method: 提出CableVLA框架，融合视觉、语言与动作信息。其中包含TopoHead模块利用视觉输入提取拓扑相关因果信息，TacSense模块通过帧与像元分支结合触觉感知，辅助以仿真推导的运动和接触事件指导学习，引入残差与门控机制优化动作预测。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真环境中，CableVLA的布线成功率由基线方法的62.6%提升至84.9%。TacSense在滑动识别等触觉任务上大幅优于同参数量的CNN-LSTM，并在不同感知器冻结下保持性能；还进行了多任务和拓扑预测评估。

Conclusion: CableVLA框架显著提升了电缆布线控制任务中的多模态感知和动作决策能力，实现了从仿真到真实设备的策略无缝迁移。

Abstract: Cable routing requires coordinated control of global cable topology and changing local contacts. We present CableVLA, an end-to-end multimodal vision-language-action framework that converts simulation-privileged supervision into deployable cable-topology and tactile representations. TopoHead distills node-level physics and current and future cable-topology information into causal visual context for the action expert. TacSense uses complementary frame and taxel branches to learn contact dynamics from resistive arrays, with simulator-derived kinematics and contact events providing supervision beyond the measured force map. A contact gate activates force-tactile residuals that refine the next 8 arm-and-gripper actions of a frozen topology-conditioned policy. Across 345 MuJoCo evaluations, CableVLA improves success from 62.6% for the $π_{0.5}$-V visual baseline to 84.9%. TacSense achieves pronounced gains in slip-transition recognition over a CNN-LSTM baseline with a similar parameter count, and this advantage persists under frozen-encoder probes. Topology prediction and 57-task tactile evaluations assess representation quality, while policy adaptation studies evaluate downstream control performance. Cross-simulator and real-robot comparisons further examine zero-shot policy transfer under changes in dynamics and sensing.

</details>


### [213] [A Deployable Four-Finger Payload for Teleoperated Free-Flying Manipulation with Astrobee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25614)
*William Su,Jordan Kam,Yunosuke Nakamura,Yixiao Wang,Jianshu Zhou,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双手远程操作管道和可部署的四指抓取器设计，用于未来地球低轨空间站自由飞行机器人完成货物搬运与维护任务，并通过虚拟现实接口进行控制。在ISS仿真环境下实现了抓取和运输的初步实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着地球低轨空间站需求增长，将需要机器人系统在有/无人值守期间完成日常任务如货物处理和维护，因此需要新的操作机构和高效的人机交互方式。

Method: 设计了具备17自由度的四指抓手，配合线性滑轨系统，采用VR设备映射地面操作员动作到机器人。使用基于MuJoCo的零重力ISS仿真器，进行Astrobee机器人带抓手的远程操作实验，搬运空间站货物包，统计多次实验数据。

Result: 通过十次实验，评估任务完成率、抓取接触持续性、耗时和货物运动特性，获得了初步的实验效果。

Conclusion: 初步实验结果表明，该方案可实现复杂的双手操控与货物搬运任务，为未来空间站机器人自动化作业奠定基础，也验证了虚拟现实控制与新型抓手设计的可行性。

Abstract: This article presents a bimanual teleoperation pipeline and conceptual design of a deployable four-finger payload for intra-vehicular free-flyers. Future habitats in low-Earth orbit (LEO) will require systems to perform mundane tasks like cargo handling and maintenance during crewed and uncrewed periods. The gripper payload provides 17 manipulation degrees-of-freedom (DoF) through four independently actuated fingers on a linear rail system. To control it, a virtual reality (VR) device interface maps the human ground operator's hand motions to the finger pairs, their separation to the rail, and common wrist motion to Astrobee translation. We present the preliminary results of teleoperating Astrobee in a custom zero-gravity MuJoCo-based International Space Station (ISS) simulator through ten repeated trials of transporting a rigid ISS Cargo Transfer Bag (CTB). We measure task success, continuous contact retention, completion time, and cargo motion.

</details>


### [214] [Relative Contact Velocity-Controlled Hand-Object Mechanism for Dexterous Tool Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25619)
*Sunyu Wang,Jean Oh,Nancy S. Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用多指机器人手工具操作方法，并在多种手型和工具上进行了仿真实验，验证了方法的通用性和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多指机器人对工具操作过程（拾取、持握、使用）的通用性与适应性不足，不同手型或工具常需重设计运动策略，限制了机器人的应用广度。为提升其通用性，受人体操作和机械设计思想启发，作者希望建立统一的操作机制与规划框架。

Method: 将机器人手与工具统一建模为“手-物体机制（HOM）”，通过若干子装配约束描述各指间的相对接触速度和力约束。基于HOM，提出了兼具物理可解释性和轻量化的运动规划与接触估计算法，采用最小二乘法及互补滤波器实现。方法在仿真中通过远程操控五种不同机器人手进行评估。

Result: 实验显示，所提方法使五种机器人手均完成了无需特定参考轨迹即可实现的完整工具操作流程，展现了较佳的灵巧动作和对不同手型、工具和任务的自适应能力。

Conclusion: 本方法基于运动学和几何学基础实现了多指机器人手通用的工具操作能力，对提升机器人灵巧度与实际应用有显著意义。

Abstract: This work investigates how to enable general multi-finger robotic hands to perform the complete tool manipulation process, which entails picking up a tool, loading it into a suitable pose, and then wielding it. Inspired by human tool manipulation and mechanical design principles, we model the hand and the tool as a unified hand-object mechanism (HOM) composed of sub-assemblies. Specifically, we define a HOM as consisting of the hand, the object, and the generalized contact frames, allowing the HOM's motions to be expressed with the same set of Cartesian-space relative contact velocities, irrespective of the hand's kinematics and geometry. Then, we define a HOM's sub-assemblies as relative contact velocity and contact force constraints between fingers. Building on these definitions, we developed a lightweight and physically interpretable motion planning and contact estimation framework using least squares and a complementary filter. We evaluated our framework in simulation by teleoperating five different robotic hands. The results show that our framework enabled all five hands to execute the complete tool manipulation process, achieving dexterous behaviors even from identical, simple reference trajectories. Furthermore, the results showcase our framework's adaptability to different hands, tools, and tasks, enabled by its kinematic and geometric foundation.

</details>


### [215] [From Instrument-Mounted Demonstrations to In-Vivo Execution: Learning Bimanual Laparoscopic Appendectomy Without Robot-Collected Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25625)
*Dongho Yee,Juahn Oh,Jinseok Lee,Jiyul Lee,Yechan Seo,Seong Jeong,Minsung Kim,Seonho Shim,Younghoon Noh,Hyuk Choi,Youngbin Kong,Kyu Eun Lee,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需外部相机或追踪器，仅靠小型传感器记录腹腔镜手术器械运动，以采集手术动作并训练手术机器人，实现了在活体动物上的自动阑尾切除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前多数微创手术仍以手持操作，手术后的器械运动数据无法保存，仅保留内窥镜视频，导致机器人模仿学习缺乏高质量动作数据。
